local function lpUnpack(t)
  return unpack(t, 1, t.n)
end

local function lpAppend(dst, off, ...)
  local p = table.pack(...)
  table.move(p, 1, p.n, off + 1, dst)
end

local function lpCopy(dst, off, src)
  for i = 1, src.n do
    dst[off + i] = src[i]
  end
  dst.n = off + src.n
end

local function lpT2(i1, i2, map, arr, arr2, env)
  return function(...)
  end
end

local LoadingScreen = function()
  local U = getgenv and getgenv() or _G;
  local q = Color3.fromRGB;
  local f = UDim2.fromOffset;
  local A = UDim2.new;
  U.ChilliCanvasHold = true;
  local b = game:GetService("CoreGui");
  pcall(function()
    local u = gethui();
    if typeof(u) == "Instance" then
      b = u;
    end
  end);
  local u = b:FindFirstChild("ChilliHubLoading");
  if u then
    u:Destroy();
  end
  local function u(c, e, P)
    local J = Instance.new(c);
    for c, x in next, P, nil do
      J[c] = x;
    end
    J.Parent = e;
    return J;
  end
  local function c(e, P)
    u("UICorner", e, {CornerRadius = UDim.new(P, P == 1 and 0 or 10)});
  end
  local e = u("ScreenGui", nil, {Name = "ChilliHubLoading", ResetOnSpawn = false, IgnoreGuiInset = true, DisplayOrder = 1000, ZIndexBehavior = Enum.ZIndexBehavior.Sibling});
  local P = u("Frame", e, {AnchorPoint = Vector2.new(0.5, 0), Position = A(0.5, 0, 0, 24), Size = f(260, 64), BackgroundColor3 = q(18, 18, 22), BackgroundTransparency = 0.05});
  c(P, 0);
  u("UIStroke", P, {Color = q(255, 60, 60), Thickness = 1.5, Transparency = 0.2});
  local function J(x, I, X, E, a, n, i)
    return u("TextLabel", P, {BackgroundTransparency = 1, Position = f(12, x), Size = A(1, -24, 0, I), Font = X, TextSize = E, TextColor3 = a, TextXAlignment = n, Text = i});
  end
  local x, I = Enum.Font.GothamBold, Enum.TextXAlignment.Left;
  J(8, 18, x, 15, q(255, 255, 255), I, "Chilli Hub");
  local X = J(8, 18, x, 15, q(255, 120, 90), Enum.TextXAlignment.Right, "0%");
  local x = J(27, 14, Enum.Font.GothamMedium, 12, q(170, 170, 185), I, "Loading part 1 / 10");
  J = u("Frame", P, {Position = f(12, 46), Size = A(1, -24, 0, 8), BackgroundColor3 = q(42, 42, 50)});
  c(J, 1);
  local f = u("Frame", J, {Size = A(0, 0, 1, 0), BackgroundColor3 = q(255, 255, 255)});
  c(f, 1);
  u("UIGradient", f, {Color = ColorSequence.new(q(255, 50, 50), q(255, 150, 60))});
  e.Parent = b;
  local b, u = "ChilliLibrary/SAE_LoadProgress.txt";
  pcall(function()
    if isfile(b) then
      u = tonumber(readfile(b):match("^(%d+)"));
    end
  end);
  local function c(P)
    pcall(function()
      if not isfolder("ChilliLibrary") then
        makefolder("ChilliLibrary");
      end
      writefile(b, P .. " " .. os.time());
    end);
  end
  c(0);
  U.ChilliHubLoading = os.clock() + 30;
  U.ChilliHubLoadStep = function(b)
    local P = math.clamp(math.floor(tonumber(b) or 0), 0, 10);
    c(P);
    pcall(function()
      f.Size, X.Text = A(P / 10, 0, 1, 0), P * 10 .. "%";
      x.Text = P >= 10 and "Loaded" or "Loading part " .. P + 1 .. " / 10";
    end);
    task.wait();
    if P < 10 then
      return;
    end
    U.ChilliHubLoadStep = nil;
    U.ChilliCanvasHold = nil;
    U.ChilliHubLoading = nil;
    if u and u < 10 then
      pcall(function()
        x.Text, x.TextColor3 = "Last load stopped at " .. u * 10 .. "% (part " .. u + 1 .. " / 10)", q(255, 170, 90);
      end);
      task.delay(8, pcall, e.Destroy, e);
    else
      pcall(e.Destroy, e);
    end
  end;
end

    local g = p({0, J(0, 36, 84)}, {1, J(0, 31, 54)});

    local V = p({0, J(255, 193, 194)}, {0.0570934266, J(255, 132, 123)}, {1, J(239, 28, 28)});

    local w, UA = P.UserInputType, P.UserInputState.Begin;
    local qA = w.MouseButton1;
    local fA = w.MouseMovement;
    local AA, bA = w.Touch, {};
    local function uA(cA, eA)
      local PA = cA:Connect(eA);
      bA[#bA + 1] = PA;
      return PA;
    end
    local function cA(eA, PA, JA)
      return uA(eA:GetPropertyChangedSignal(PA), JA);
    end
    local eA, PA, JA = J(198, 198, 198), {TextLabel = 1, TextButton = 1, TextBox = 1}, {n = "Name", A = "AnchorPoint", P = "Position", S = "Size", B = "BackgroundColor3", T = "BackgroundTransparency", Z = "ZIndex", O = "LayoutOrder", X = "TextXAlignment", W = "TextWrapped", V = "Visible", C = "ClipsDescendants", R = "Rotation", H = "HorizontalAlignment", Y = "SortOrder", a = "Active", t = "TextTransparency", y = "TextYAlignment", PH = "PlaceholderText", PC3 = "PlaceholderColor3", G = "Padding", x = "Text"};
    local function xA(IA, XA)
      local EA = Instance.new(IA);
      if PA[IA] then
        EA.BackgroundTransparency = 1;
        EA.Text = "";
      end
      if IA == "TextButton" then
        EA.AutoButtonColor = false;
      elseif IA == "ImageButton" then
        EA.AutoButtonColor = false;
      end
      if IA == "TextBox" then
        EA.ClearTextOnFocus = false;
      end
      if EA:IsA("GuiObject") then
        EA.BorderSizePixel = 0;
      end
      for PA, IA in next, XA, nil do
        if type(PA) == "number" then
          IA.Parent = EA;
        elseif PA ~= "p" then
          if PA ~= "Parent" then
            EA[JA[PA] or PA] = IA;
          end
        end
      end
      EA.Parent = XA.p or XA.Parent;
      return EA;
    end
    local function PA(JA)
      return xA("Frame", JA);
    end
    local function JA(IA)
      return x(1, 0, 0, IA);
    end
    local function IA(XA)
      return xA("TextButton", XA);
    end
    local function XA(EA, aA, nA, iA)
      local MA = xA("UIStroke", {Thickness = EA, Color = aA or i, ApplyStrokeMode = nA and B or j, LineJoinMode = P.LineJoinMode.Round, Transparency = iA or 0});
      pcall(function()
        MA.BorderOffset = a(0, 0);
        MA.BorderStrokePosition = P.BorderStrokePosition.Outer;
        MA.StrokeSizingMode = P.StrokeSizingMode.ScaledSize;
      end);
      return MA;
    end
    local function EA(aA, nA)

    local function aA(nA)

    local function nA(iA)
      iA.FontFace = Z;
      iA.TextColor3 = n;
      iA.TextScaled = true;
      iA.LineHeight = 1;
      XA(0.08, i, true).Parent = iA;
      return iA;
    end
    local function iA(MA, YA)
      MA.FontFace = YA;
      MA.TextColor3 = n;
      MA.TextScaled = true;
      MA.LineHeight = 1;
      EA(_, "TextStyleGradient").Parent = MA;
      YA = XA(0.065, n, true);
      EA(g, "StrokeStyleGradient").Parent = YA;
      YA.Parent = MA;
      return MA;
    end
    local function MA(YA, kA)
      local yA = xA("TextLabel", YA);
      if kA then
        return iA(yA, kA == true and Z or kA);
      end
      return nA(yA);
    end
    local function iA(YA)
      YA.TextTransparency = 0.32;
      local kA = YA:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke");
      kA.Thickness = 0.06;
      kA.Transparency = 0.2;
      return YA;
    end
    local function YA(kA, yA, rA, mA, WA)
      local LA = A:Create(kA, TweenInfo.new(rA or 0.15, mA or P.EasingStyle.Quad, WA or P.EasingDirection.Out), yA);
      LA:Play();
      return LA;
    end
    local function A(kA, yA, rA)
      return YA(kA, {BackgroundTransparency = yA}, rA);
    end
    local function kA(yA)
      local rA = yA.UserInputType;
      return rA == qA or rA == AA;
    end
    local function yA(rA)
      return E(rA.Position.X, rA.Position.Y);
    end
    local function rA(mA, WA, LA, NA)
      local DA, KA;
      for dA, dA in ipairs(mA) do
        uA(dA.InputBegan, function(mA)
          if not DA then
            if mA.UserInputState == UA then
              if kA(mA) then
                if WA(mA) ~= false then
                  local WA;
                  WA, DA = mA.UserInputType == AA and mA or nil, true;
                  KA = WA;
                end
              end
            end
          end
        end);
      end
      local function mA()
        DA = false;
      end
      local WA, dA = uA(b.InputChanged, function(tA)
        if DA then
          if KA and tA == KA then
            LA(tA);
          elseif not KA then
            if tA.UserInputType == fA then
              LA(tA);
            end
          end
        end
      end), b.InputEnded;
      local function LA(tA)
        local CA = DA and (tA.UserInputType == qA or tA == KA);
        if CA then
          DA, KA = false, nil;
          if NA then
            NA();
          end
        end
      end
      return mA, WA, uA(dA, LA);
    end
    q.errs = {};
    local function mA(WA, ...)
      if WA then
        local LA, NA = pcall(WA, ...);
        if not LA then
          warn("[Chilli] " .. l(NA));
          if #q.errs < 50 then
            q.errs[#q.errs + 1] = l(NA);
          end
        end
      end
    end
    local function WA(LA, NA, DA)
      local KA, dA, tA = xA("UIScale", {p = LA, n = "InteractionScale"}), false;
      local function CA(sA, lA)
        if tA then
          tA:Cancel();
        end
        tA = YA(KA, {Scale = sA}, lA);
      end
      uA(LA.MouseEnter, function()
        dA = true;
        CA(NA, 0.1);
      end);
      uA(LA.MouseLeave, function()
        dA = false;
        CA(1, 0.1);
      end);
      uA(LA.MouseButton1Down, function()
        CA(DA, 0.055);
      end);
      uA(LA.MouseButton1Up, function()
        CA(dA and NA or 1, 0.08);
      end);
    end
    G, w = setmetatable, {__mode = "k"};
    local LA = G({}, w);
    local function NA(DA, KA)
      local dA, tA = DA.AbsolutePosition, DA.AbsoluteSize;
      return DA.Parent and DA.Visible and KA.X >= dA.X and KA.X <= dA.X + tA.X and KA.Y >= dA.Y and KA.Y <= dA.Y + tA.Y;
    end
    uA(b.InputChanged, function(DA)
      local KA = LA[DA];
      if KA then
        KA.p = yA(DA);
        if (KA.p - KA.s).Magnitude > 10 then
          KA.x = true;
        end
      end
    end);
    local function DA(KA, dA)
      KA.Active = true;
      uA(KA.InputBegan, function(tA)
        if tA.UserInputType == AA then
          if tA.UserInputState == UA then
            local CA = yA(tA);
            LA[tA] = {b = KA, s = CA, p = CA};
          end
        end
      end);
      uA(KA.Activated, function(tA)
        if tA then
          if tA.UserInputType == AA then
            local CA = LA[tA];
            if CA then
              if CA.b ~= KA then
                return;
              elseif CA.x then
                return;
              elseif not NA(KA, CA.p) then
                return;
              end
            else
              return;
            end
          end
        end
        mA(dA);
      end);
    end
    local LA = {};
    uA(u.Heartbeat, function()
      if #LA > 0 then
        local KA = LA;
        LA = {};
        for dA, dA in ipairs(KA) do
          mA(dA);
        end
      end
    end);
    q.q = function(KA)
      local dA = LA;
      dA[#dA + 1] = KA;
    end;
    local function LA(KA, dA, tA, CA, sA, lA)
      local SA, GA = PA({p = KA, n = "Caret", A = Y, P = tA, S = CA, T = 1, Z = sA, aA(true)}), {
        {0.335355, 45},
        {0.664645, -45}
      };
      for KA, KA in ipairs(GA) do
        PA({p = SA, n = "Arm", A = Y, P = I(KA[1], 0.535355), R = KA[2], S = I(0.6657, lA or 0.2), B = dA, Z = sA + 1});
      end
      return SA;
    end
    local function KA(dA)
      local tA = IA({p = dA.p, n = dA.n, A = Y, P = dA.P or m, S = dA.S, O = dA.O or 0, Z = dA.Z or 99});
      local CA = PA({p = tA, n = "Main", A = Y, P = m, S = I(1, dA.full and 1 or 0.92), B = J(175, 0, 0), XA(dA.full and 0.07 or 0.06)});
      local sA = PA({p = CA, n = "ColorFrame", A = r, P = W, S = I(1, 0.9), B = n, EA(V, "RedGradient")});
      local lA, SA = PA({p = sA, n = "Transparent", A = Y, P = m, S = I(0.965, 0.88), B = n, Z = 2, EA(F, "RedGradient")}), MA({p = sA, n = "Label", A = Y, P = m, S = dA.LSize or I(0.94, 0.72), x = dA.Text, W = dA.wrap == true, Z = 6}, true);
      WA(tA, dA.h or 1.035, 0.96);
      return tA, CA, SA, sA, lA;
    end
    local function dA(tA, CA, sA, lA, SA, GA)
      local ZA = PA({p = tA, n = "Switch", A = E(CA, 0.5), P = I(sA, 0.5), S = I(lA, SA), B = n, Z = GA, XA(0.06)});
      local tA = xA("UIGradient", {p = ZA, n = "OFF", Color = ColorSequence.new(i), R = 90, Transparency = NumberSequence.new(0.6)});
      local CA = EA(R, "ON");
      CA.Parent = ZA;
      local sA = PA({p = ZA, n = "Hold", A = Y, S = I(0.72, 0.72), B = J(89, 89, 89), Z = GA + 2, XA(0.09), aA(), PA({n = "Color", A = r, P = W, S = I(1, 0.9), B = n, Z = GA + 3})});
      return ZA, function(lA, SA)
        tA.Enabled = not lA;
        CA.Enabled = lA;
        local tA = I(lA and 0.72 or 0.28, 0.5);
        if SA == false then
          sA.Position = tA;
        else
          YA(sA, {Position = tA}, 0.12);
        end
      end;
    end
    q.N = xA;
    q.tw = YA;
    local function tA(CA)
      return type(CA) == "table" and table.clone(CA) or CA;
    end
    local function CA(sA, lA, SA, GA)
      local function ZA(oA)
        A(lA, oA and 0.42 or 0.6, 0.16);
        YA(SA, {Size = I(oA and 1 or 0, 0.11)}, 0.2, O);
      end
      uA(sA.Focused, function()
        ZA(true);
      end);
      uA(sA.FocusLost, function()
        ZA(false);
        if GA then
          GA();
        end
      end);
    end
    local function sA(lA)
      local SA, GA = pcall(readfile, lA);
      return SA and GA or nil;
    end
    local function lA(SA, GA)
      pcall(writefile, SA, GA);
    end
    local function SA(GA)
      if isfolder then
        if not isfolder(GA) then
          pcall(makefolder, GA);
        end
      end
    end
    local function GA(ZA)
      if not ZA then
        return;
      end
      local oA, zA = pcall(c.JSONDecode, c, ZA);
      return oA and zA or nil;
    end
    local function ZA(oA)
      local zA, pA = pcall(c.JSONEncode, c, oA);
      return zA and pA or nil;
    end
    q.dec = GA;
    q.enc = ZA;
    q.rd = sA;
    q.wr = lA;
    local oA, zA, pA = 1000, 3, 12;
    local function _A(gA)
      if gA <= 0 then
        return 0;
      end
      local RA = 10 ^ (D(math.log10(gA)) - 2);
      return D(gA / RA + 0.5) * RA;
    end
    local function gA(RA)
      RA = K(tonumber(RA) or 0, 0, oA);
      return RA <= 0 and 0 or _A(10 ^ (zA + (pA - zA) * RA / oA));
    end
    local function _A(RA)
      RA = tonumber(RA) or 0;
      if RA <= 0 then
        return 0;
      end
      return K(D((math.log10(RA) - zA) / (pA - zA) * oA * 100 + 0.5) / 100, 0, oA);
    end
    local function zA(pA)
      local RA, VA = string.format, pA >= 100 and "%.0f" or pA >= 10 and "%.1f";
      local FA = RA(VA or "%.2f", pA);
      return if FA:find(".", 1, true) then FA:gsub("0+$", ""):gsub("%.$", "") else FA;
    end
    local function pA(RA)
      local VA = gA(RA);
      if VA <= 0 then
        return "Off";
      elseif VA < 1000000 then
        return zA(VA / 1000) .. " K/s";
      elseif VA < 1000000000 then
        return zA(VA / 1000000) .. " M/s";
      end
      return zA(VA / 1000000000) .. " B/s";
    end
    local function RA(VA)
      local FA = gA(VA);
      if FA <= 0 then
        return "0";
      elseif FA < 1000000 then
        return zA(FA / 1000) .. "k";
      end

    _A.Collect = function(R)
      local T, w = {}, {};
      for EA, aA in ipairs(R.Opts) do
        EA = aA.NoSave;
        local iA, SA = not EA;
        if EA then
          SA = iA;

          end
        end
        if SA then
          T[aA.Tab] = T[aA.Tab] or {};
          T[aA.Tab][aA.Sec] = T[aA.Tab][aA.Sec] or {};
          T[aA.Tab][aA.Sec][aA.Name] = {Type = aA.Type, Value = aA.Value, Unit = aA.Unit};
        end
      end
      for EA, aA in next, R.St, nil do
        w[EA] = {Value = aA.Value};
      end
      return {Version = 2, Profile = R.Active, SavedAt = os.time(), States = w, Values = T};
    end;

      end
      lA(z(R, T), EA);
      lA(z(R, T) .. ".bak", EA);
      return true;
    end;
    _A.Dirty = function(R)
      if R.AutoSave then
        if R.busy then
          return;
        elseif not R.ready then
          return;
        end
      else
        return;
      end
      R.saveN = (R.saveN or 0) + 1;
      local T = R.saveN;
      task.delay(0.35, function()
        if R.saveN == T then
          if R.AutoSave then
            R:Save();
          end
        end
      end);
    end;
    local function R(T, w, EA)
      for aA, iA in next, T, nil do
        aA = type(iA) == "table" and iA[w];
        if type(aA) == "table" then
          if aA[EA] ~= nil then
            return aA[EA];
          end
        end
      end
    end
    _A.Apply = function(T, w)
      if type(w) ~= "table" then
        return false;
      end
      T.busy = true;
      local EA = type(w.Values) == "table" and w.Values or {};
      if T.pre then
        mA(T.pre, EA);
      end
      for aA, iA in ipairs(T.Opts) do
        if not iA.Skip then
          if iA.Type ~= "button" then
            if iA.Type ~= "text" then
              aA = EA[iA.Tab] and EA[iA.Tab][iA.Sec] and EA[iA.Tab][iA.Sec][iA.Name] or R(EA, iA.Sec, iA.Name);
              local R = if type(aA) == "table" then aA.Value else nil;
              R = if R == nil then tA(iA.Def) else R;
              if R ~= nil then
                pcall(iA.Set, iA, R);
              end
            end
          end
        end
      end
      for R, aA in next, type(w.States) == "table" and w.States or {}, nil do
        EA = T.St[R];
        if EA and type(aA) == "table" and aA.Value ~= nil then
          EA.Value = aA.Value;
          for w, w in ipairs(EA.ls) do
            mA(w, aA.Value);
          end
        elseif type(aA) == "table" then
          T:State(R, aA.Value);
        end
      end
      T.busy = false;
      return true;
    end;
    _A.Find = function(R, T)
      for w, w in ipairs(R:List()) do
        if w:lower() == l(T):lower() then
          return w;
        end
      end
    end;
    _A.Load = function(R, T)
      local w = R:Find(T);
      if not w then
        return false, "Config does not exist: " .. l(T);
      end
      local function EA(aA)
        return type(aA) == "table" and (type(aA.Values) == "table" or type(aA.States) == "table");
      end
      T = GA(sA(z(R, w)));
      if EA(T) then
      else
        T = GA(sA(z(R, w) .. ".bak"));
        if EA(T) then
          lA(z(R, w), ZA(T) or "");
        end
      end
      if not EA(T) then
        return false, "Config file is empty or invalid.";
      end
      R.Active = w;
      R:Apply(T);
      if R.AutoSave then
        if R.ready then
          R:Save();
        end
      end
      mA(R.cfgNotes);
      return true;
    end;
    _A.List = function(R)
      local T, w, EA, aA = {[e:lower()] = e}, {}, pcall(listfiles, R.dirP .. "/configs");
      for iA, iA in ipairs(EA and aA or {}) do
        R = l(iA):gsub("\\", "/"):match("([^/]+)%.json$");
        if R then
          if R ~= "" then
            T[R:lower()] = R;
          end
        end
      end
      for R, EA in next, T, nil do
        if R ~= e:lower() then
          w[#w + 1] = EA;
        end
      end
      table.sort(w, function(R, T)
        return R:lower() < T:lower();
      end);
      table.insert(w, 1, e);
      return w;
    end;
    _A.Mgr = function(R, T)
      local w = R.dirP .. "/config_state.json";
      if T then
        lA(w, ZA({Version = 2, AutoLoad = R.AutoLoad, AutoSave = R.AutoSave, StartupConfig = R.Startup}) or "{}");
        return;
      end
      local EA, aA = sA("ChilliLibrary/config_state.json"), sA(w);
      if (if aA then not aA else EA) and EA ~= "" then
        local aA, iA = pcall(listfiles, "ChilliLibrary/configs");
        for SA, oA in ipairs(aA and iA or {}) do
          T = l(oA):gsub("\\", "/"):match("([^/]+)$");
          SA = T and T:sub(-5) == ".json" and sA(oA);
          if SA then
            lA(R.dirP .. "/configs/" .. T, SA);
          end
        end
        lA(w, EA);
      end
      EA = GA(sA(w)) or {};
      R.AutoLoad, R.AutoSave, R.Startup = EA.AutoLoad ~= false, EA.AutoSave ~= false, l(EA.StartupConfig or e);
      R.Active = R.Startup;
    end;
    _A.Default = function(e)
      e.busy = true;
      for R, R in ipairs(e.Opts) do
        if not R.Skip then
          if R.Def ~= nil then
            if R.Tab ~= "Config" then
              pcall(R.Set, R, tA(R.Def));
            end
          end
        end
      end
      for R, R in next, e.St, nil do
        if R.Def ~= nil then
          R:Set(tA(R.Def));
        end
      end
      e.busy = false;
      e:Dirty();
    end;
    _A.Float = function(e)
      local R = e;
      local T = OA({DisplayOrder = 59});
      R.fgui = T;
      local w = PA({p = T, A = k, P = x(0, 16, 0.3, 0), S = X(56, 56), T = 1});
      local EA = xA("UIScale", {p = w});
      e = xA("ImageButton", {p = w, A = Y, P = m, S = L, T = 1, Image = "rbxassetid://128961717706452", ScaleType = P.ScaleType.Fit, xA("UICorner", {CornerRadius = a(0.28, 0)})});
      local aA = xA("UIScale", {p = e});
      local function iA()
        EA.Scale = K(c().X * 0.035 / 56, 0.7, 1.4);
      end
      iA();
      if workspace.CurrentCamera then
        cA(workspace.CurrentCamera, "ViewportSize", iA);
      end
      local EA, iA, tA, lA, SA;
      local function oA(zA, pA)
        if EA == "mouse" then
          return zA.UserInputType == (pA and fA or qA);
        end
        pA = EA;
        return zA == pA;
      end
      local function qA()
        YA(aA, {Scale = 1}, 0.14, P.EasingStyle.Back);
      end
      uA(e.InputBegan, function(fA)
        if kA(fA) then
          if fA.UserInputState ~= UA then
            return;
          elseif EA then
            return;
          end
        else
          return;
        end
        local UA, kA, zA = fA.UserInputType == AA and fA or "mouse", yA(fA), w.Position;
        EA, iA, tA, lA, SA = UA, kA, zA, false, false;
        YA(aA, {Scale = 0.9}, 0.08);
      end);
      uA(b.InputChanged, function(UA)
        if EA then
          if not oA(UA, true) then
            return;
          end
        else
          return;
        end
        local fA = yA(UA) - iA;
        if not lA then
          if fA.Magnitude < 8 then
            return;
          end
          lA, SA = true, true;
          qA();
        end
        local UA, AA = T.AbsoluteSize, w.AbsoluteSize;
        local aA, iA = tA.X.Offset + tA.X.Scale * UA.X + fA.X, tA.Y.Offset + tA.Y.Scale * UA.Y + fA.Y;
        w.Position = X(K(aA, 0, d(0, UA.X - AA.X)), K(iA, AA.Y * 0.5, d(AA.Y * 0.5, UA.Y - AA.Y * 0.5)));
      end);
      uA(b.InputEnded, function(w)
        if EA then
          if oA(w, false) then
            EA, lA = nil, false;
            qA();
          end
        end
      end);
      uA(e.Activated, function()
        if SA then
          SA = false;
          return;
        end
        R:Show(not R.open);
      end);
      T.Parent = R.par;
    end;
    local e, R = {Zero = "0", One = "1", Two = "2", Three = "3", Four = "4", Five = "5", Six = "6", Seven = "7", Eight = "8", Nine = "9", LeftControl = "LCtrl", RightControl = "RCtrl", LeftShift = "LShift", RightShift = "RShift", LeftAlt = "LAlt", RightAlt = "RAlt", Return = "Enter", Space = "Space", Backspace = "Back"}, {"Zero", "One", "Two", "Three", "Four", "Five", "Six", "Seven", "Eight", "Nine"};
    for T, w in ipairs(R) do
      e["Keypad" .. w] = "Num" .. T - 1;
    end
    local function R(T)
      return T and (e[T] or l(T)) or nil;
    end
    local e, T = {toggle = true, button = true, slider = true, input = true}, {toggle = true, button = true};
    local function w(UA)
      return e[UA.Type] and UA.Quick ~= false and not UA.mirrorOf;
    end
    local function e(UA)
      return type(UA) == "table" and table.clone(UA) or {};
    end
    _A.Quick = function(UA)
      local qA = UA;
      local fA = qA.QuickDefaults;
      local AA = qA:State("Quick Pinned Features", e(fA.pins));
      local EA = qA:State("Quick Pin Groups", e(fA.groups));
      local aA = qA:State("Quick Keybinds", e(fA.keys));
      local iA = qA:State("Quick Panel Position", {});
      local kA = qA:State("Quick Panel Collapsed", {});
      local tA = qA:State("Quick Open Key", "LeftControl");
      qA:State("Quick LeftCenter Hidden", fA.hud == true);
      local function lA()
        return type(aA.Value) == "table" and aA.Value or {};
      end
      local function SA()
        return type(AA.Value) == "table" and AA.Value or {};
      end
      local function oA(zA)
        return K(D(tonumber(type(EA.Value) == "table" and EA.Value[zA]) or 1), 1, 5);
      end
      local function zA(pA)
        return table.find(SA(), pA) ~= nil;
      end
      local pA = OA({p = qA.par, DisplayOrder = 5, Enabled = false});
      qA.qgui = pA;
      local gA, RA = (H and 150 or 120) / 100, {true, true, true, true, true};
      local function VA(FA)
        local vA = 0.078 * gA * 0.97;
        return K(FA, 0, d(0, 0.998 - vA));
      end
      local function FA(vA)
        local hA, OA = (H and {0.023, 0.023, 0.42} or {0.023, 0.303, 0.7})[vA], 0.078 * gA;
        if vA == 2 then
          if H then
            return 1 - OA * 0.97 + OA * 0.03 - OA * 0.1 - OA * 0.97, hA;
          end
        end
        if vA <= 3 then
          return 1, hA;
        end
        if vA == 4 then
          return 0.11, 0.5;
        end
        if H then
          return 0.11 + OA * 0.97 + OA * 0.1 - OA * 0.03, 0.5;
        end
        return 0.11, 0.74;
      end
      local function vA(hA)
        local OA, jA = FA(hA);
        local FA = type(iA.Value) == "table" and iA.Value[l(hA)];
        if type(FA) == "table" then
          OA, jA = tonumber(FA.X or FA[1]) or OA, tonumber(FA.Y or FA[2]) or jA;
        else
        end
        return VA(OA), K(jA, 0, 0.98);
      end
      local FA, hA = {}, {};
      local function OA(jA)
        return type(kA.Value) == "table" and kA.Value[l(jA)] == true;
      end
      local function jA()
        for wA, Ua in ipairs(hA) do
          Ua.col = OA(wA);
        end
      end
      for wA = 1, 5, 1 do
        local Ua = PA({p = pA, n = "QuickBar" .. wA, T = 1, S = I(0.078, 0.032), V = false, Z = 2});
        local qa = IA({p = Ua, n = "Handle", a = true, A = r, P = W, S = I(0.94, 1), B = i, T = 0.35, Z = 3, XA(0.06), PA({n = "HeaderAccent", A = E(0.5, 1), P = I(0.5, 1), S = I(1, 0.07), B = n, T = 0.08, Z = 4})});
        MA({p = qa, n = "Title", A = k, P = I(0.07, 0.5), S = I(0.62, 0.55), x = "Quick Bar " .. wA, X = Q, Z = 4}, true);
        local fa = PA({p = Ua, n = "Body", A = r, P = I(0.5, 1), S = I(1.32, 0), T = 1, C = true, Z = 2, xA("UIPadding", {n = "BodyPadding", PaddingLeft = a(0.03, 0), PaddingRight = a(0.03, 0)})});
        local Aa, ba = xA("UIListLayout", {p = fa, n = "BodyLayout", H = v, Y = S}), IA({p = qa, n = "Collapse", a = true, A = y, P = I(0.955, 0.5), S = I(0.17, 0.6), Z = 4});
        local v = LA(ba, n, m, I(0.85, 0.85), 4);
        local ua = {
          pn = Ua,
          hd = qa,
          body = fa,
          bl = Aa,
          car = v,
          items = {},
          cons = {},
          frac = 0
        };
        ua.col = OA(wA);
        local function OA()
          ua.col = not ua.col;
          local Aa = e(kA.Value);
          Aa[l(wA)] = ua.col;
          kA:Set(Aa);
          if ua.tw then
            ua.tw:Cancel();
          end
          ua.tw = YA(fa, {Size = I(1.32, ua.col and 0 or ua.frac)}, 0.26, O);
          YA(v, {Rotation = ua.col and 180 or 0}, 0.26, O);
        end
        DA(ba, OA);
        local v, fa, Aa;
        rA({qa}, function(ca)
          local ea = yA(ca);
          if NA(qa, ea) then
            if NA(ba, ea) then
              return false;
            end
          else
            return false;
          end
          ca = nil;
          ca, v = E(Ua.Position.X.Scale, Ua.Position.Y.Scale), ea;
          fa, Aa = ca, false;
        end, function(NA)
          local qa = pA.AbsoluteSize;
          if qa.X <= 0 then
            return;
          end
          local ba = yA(NA) - v;
          if not Aa then
            if ba.Magnitude < 6 then
              return;
            end
          end
          Aa = true;
          Ua.Position = I(VA(fa.X + ba.X / qa.X), K(fa.Y + ba.Y / qa.Y, 0, 0.98));
        end, function()
          if Aa then
            local v = e(iA.Value);
            for yA, NA in next, v, nil do
              if type(NA) ~= "table" then
                v[yA] = nil;
              end
            end
            v[l(wA)] = {X = Ua.Position.X.Scale, Y = Ua.Position.Y.Scale};
            iA:Set(v);
          else
            OA();
          end
        end);
        hA[wA] = ua;
      end
      local function v(yA, NA, VA, OA)
        local wA = xA(OA and "TextButton" or "Frame", {p = yA.body, n = "Item" .. NA, a = OA == true, O = NA, S = I(0.7121, VA), B = i, T = 0.4, Z = 3, XA(0.05)});
        yA.items[#yA.items + 1] = wA;
        return wA;
      end
      local function yA(NA, VA)
        if VA then
          MA({p = NA, n = "BarKeyChip", A = k, P = I(0.025, 0.485), S = I(0.153, 0.612), x = "[" .. VA .. "]", X = Q, Z = 6}).TextColor3 = J(190, 190, 190);
        end
      end
      local NA, VA = setmetatable, {__mode = "k"};
      local OA = NA({}, VA);
      local function wA(Ua, qa, fa)
        OA[qa] = OA[qa] or {};
        OA[qa][fa] = true;
        Ua.cons[#Ua.cons + 1] = {
          Disconnect = function()
            if OA[qa] then
              OA[qa][fa] = nil;
            end
          end
        };
      end
      local function Ua(qa, fa, Aa, ba, ua)
        local ca = fa.Type;
        if ca == "toggle" then
          local ea = v(qa, ba, ua, true);
          MA({p = ea, n = "Label", A = k, P = I(Aa and 0.19 or 0.03, 0.5), S = I(Aa and 0.52 or 0.68, 0.72), x = fa.Disp or fa.Name, Z = 4});
          yA(ea, Aa);
          local Pa, Pa = dA(ea, 1, 0.955, 0.23, 0.56, 6);
          Pa(fa.Value == true, false);
          wA(qa, fa, function()
            Pa(fa.Value == true);
          end);
          DA(ea, function()
            fa:Set(not fa.Value);
          end);
          FA[fa.Path] = ea;
        elseif ca == "button" then
          local ea, Pa, Pa = KA({p = qa.body, n = "Item" .. ba, O = ba, S = I(0.7121, ua), Z = 3, Text = fa.Disp or fa.Name});
          Pa.Position, Pa.Size = I(Aa and 0.58 or 0.5, 0.5), I(Aa and 0.78 or 0.94, 0.68);
          yA(ea, Aa);
          qa.items[#qa.items + 1] = ea;
          DA(ea, function()
            fa:Press();
          end);
          FA[fa.Path] = ea;
        elseif ca == "slider" then
          local yA = v(qa, ba, ua);
          MA({p = yA, n = "Label", A = k, P = I(0.035, 0.255), S = I(0.67, 0.34), x = fa.Disp or fa.Name, X = Q, y = P.TextYAlignment.Center, Z = 4});
          local KA = fa.row.vb:Clone();
          local Aa = fa.row.tr:Clone();
          KA.AnchorPoint, KA.Position, KA.Size = Y, I(0.84, 0.255), I(0.25, 0.34);
          KA.ZIndex = 5;
          KA.Parent = yA;
          Aa.Position, Aa.Size = I(0.5, 0.735), I(0.92, 0.24);
          Aa.ZIndex = 4;
          Aa.Parent = yA;
          local yA = Aa.Bar;
          local ea = Aa.Hold;
          local function Pa()
            ea.Size = X(d(1, Aa.AbsoluteSize.Y * 1.38), d(1, Aa.AbsoluteSize.Y * 1.38));
          end
          Pa();
          qa.cons[#qa.cons + 1] = cA(Aa, "AbsoluteSize", Pa);
          local Pa, Ja = fa:Places();
          local function xa()
            local Ia = K(fa.Max > fa.Min and K((fa.Value - fa.Min) / (fa.Max - fa.Min), 0, 1) or 0, 0.0575, 0.9425);
            yA.Size, ea.Position = I(Ia, 1), I(Ia, 0.5);
            if not KA:IsFocused() then
              KA.RichText, KA.Text = false, Pa and string.format("%." .. Ja .. "f", fa.Value) or l(math.round(fa.Value));
            end
          end
          xa();
          wA(qa, fa, xa);
          uA(KA.FocusLost, function()
            local Pa = tonumber(KA.Text);
            if Pa then
              fa:Set(Pa);
            end
            xa();
          end);
          local function KA(Pa)
            fa:Set(fa.Min + (fa.Max - fa.Min) * K((Pa.Position.X - Aa.AbsolutePosition.X) / d(Aa.AbsoluteSize.X, 1), 0, 1));
            xa();
          end
          local Pa, Pa, Ja = rA({Aa, yA, ea}, KA, KA);
          qa.cons[#qa.cons + 1] = Pa;
          qa.cons[#qa.cons + 1] = Ja;
        elseif ca == "input" then
          local yA = v(qa, ba, ua);
          MA({p = yA, n = "Label", A = k, P = I(0.035, 0.5), S = I(0.43, 0.7), x = fa.Disp or fa.Name, X = Q, Z = 4});
          local v = fa.row.fd:Clone();
          v.AnchorPoint, v.Position, v.Size = Y, I(0.72, 0.5), I(0.48, 0.62);
          v.ZIndex = 4;
          v.Parent = yA;
          local rA = v.Input;
          yA = v.Underline;
          rA.Text = l(fa.Value or "");
          CA(rA, v, yA, function()
            fa:Set(rA.Text);
            rA.Text = l(fa.Value or "");
          end);
          wA(qa, fa, function()
            if not rA:IsFocused() then
              rA.Text = l(fa.Value or "");
            end
          end);
        end
      end
      local function v()
        table.clear(FA);
        local yA, rA = lA(), {};
        for KA = 1, 5, 1 do
          rA[KA] = {};
        end
        for KA, CA in ipairs(SA()) do
          KA = qA.ByPath[CA];
          if KA then
            if w(KA) then
              local SA, wA, qa = table.insert, rA[oA(CA)], {o = KA};
              qa.k = b.KeyboardEnabled and R(yA[CA]) or nil;
              SA(wA, qa);
            end
          end
        end
        for KA, CA in ipairs(hA) do
          for SA, SA in ipairs(CA.cons) do
            SA:Disconnect();
          end
          for SA, SA in ipairs(CA.items) do
            SA:Destroy();
          end
          table.clear(CA.cons);
          table.clear(CA.items);
          local SA, wA = rA[KA], RA[KA];
          if if wA then #SA == 0 else not wA then
            CA.pn.Visible = false;
          else
            local rA, wA, qa = 0.032 * gA, 0.022 * gA, 0.005 * gA;
            yA = (#SA - 1) * qa + #SA * rA;
            local fa = wA + qa + yA;
            CA.frac = yA / fa;
            if CA.tw then
              CA.tw:Cancel();
              CA.tw = nil;
            end
            CA.pn.Size = I(0.078 * gA, fa);
            CA.hd.Size = I(0.94, wA / fa);
            CA.body.Position, CA.body.Size = I(0.5, (wA + qa) / fa), I(1.32, CA.col and 0 or CA.frac);
            CA.bl.Padding = a(qa / yA, 0);
            CA.car.Rotation = CA.col and 180 or 0;
            for wA, qa in ipairs(SA) do
              Ua(CA, qa.o, qa.k, wA, rA / yA);
            end
            CA.pn.Position = I(vA(KA));
            CA.pn.Visible = true;
          end
        end
      end
      qA.qRefresh = function(yA)
        local rA = OA[yA];
        if rA then
          for yA in next, rA, nil do
            mA(yA);
          end
        end
      end;
      local function yA(rA)
        local KA = FA[rA];
        local rA = KA and (KA:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIScale") or xA("UIScale", {p = KA}));
        if not rA then
          return;
        end
        YA(rA, {Scale = 0.94}, 0.055);
        task.delay(0.07, function()
          if rA.Parent then
            YA(rA, {Scale = 1}, 0.09);
          end
        end);
      end
      NA, VA = qA:Tab("Quick", "Right", "Quick & Keys"), qA:Tab("Settings", "Right");
      qA.setTab = VA;
      UA = VA:Section("Interface", true);
      qA.sizeOpt = UA:Slider("UI Size", 60, 160, 100, function(rA)
        qA:Scale(rA / 100);
      end, "%", 1, "Scales the main window; the corner grip does the same by hand");
      qA.sizeOpt.Quick = false;
      local rA, KA = NA:Section("Pin Pad", true, "Quick Access"), NA:Section("Pin & Key List", true, "Quick Bar & Keybinds");
      local function CA()
        AA:Set(e(fA.pins));
        EA:Set(e(fA.groups));
        iA:Set({});
      end
      VA = KA:Button("Reset Pins", "Reset", CA, "Restore default items, bars and positions", "Done!");

      local function G(o)
        o = o:match("^%s*(.-)%s*$");
        if o == "" then
          return;
        elseif #o > 48 then
          return;
        elseif o:find("[<>:\"/\\|%?%*%c]") then
          return;
        elseif o:find("[%. ]$") then
          return;
        end
        local O = o:upper():gsub("%..*$", "");
        if O == "CON" then
          return;
        elseif O == "PRN" then
          return;
        elseif O == "AUX" then
          return;
        elseif O == "NUL" then
          return;
        elseif O == "CLOCK$" then
          return;
        elseif O:match("^COM%d$") then
          return;
        elseif O:match("^LPT%d$") then
          return;
        end
        return o;
      end
      y:Button("Create New Config", "Create New", function()

          local w = 1;
          O = "Default " .. w;
          while e:Find(O) do
            w += 1;
            O = "Default " .. w;
          end
        else
          O = G(o);
          if O then
            if e:Find(O) then
              return;
            end
          else
            return;
          end
        end
        o = table.pack(e:Collect());

              end

            end
          else
          end
        end

    _A.Finish = function(U)
      local b = U;
      b.ready = true;
      mA(function()
        if not (isfile and isfile(z(b, "Default"))) then
          b:Save("Default", b:Collect());
        end
        local U = b.Startup;
        if not b:Find(U) then
          b.Startup = "Default";
        end
        b.Active = b.Startup;
      end);
      if b.AutoLoad then
        mA(function()
          for U = 1, 6, 1 do
            U = b.Startup;
            if b:Load(U) then
              break;
            end
            task.wait(0.4);
          end
        end);
      end
      b.busy = false;
      mA(b.qSync);
      mA(b.cfgNotes);
      mA(function()
        if b.sizeOpt.Value ~= 100 then
          b:Scale(b.sizeOpt.Value / 100);
        end
      end);
      mA(function()
        b:Select(b.Tabs[1]);
      end);
      b.gui.Enabled = true;
      task.spawn(function()
        for U = 1, 5, 1 do
          u.Heartbeat:Wait();
        end
        local U = b.St["Open On Launch"];
        if U then
          if U.Value ~= false then
            b:Show(true);
          end
        else
          b:Show(true);
        end
      end);
    end;

      end
    end;

      local G = xA("UIScale", {p = L});

end

                  end
                else
                  vA = LA;
                end
              end
              if vA then
                local WA = lA(hA, FA, pA, sA, RA, CA);
                if GA then
                  oA[#oA + 1] = WA;
                elseif WA.tg then
                  local LA = b[25];

                    oA[#oA + 1] = WA;
                  elseif WA.kind == "wisp" then
                    oA[#oA + 1] = WA;
                  elseif WA.kind == "pick" then
                    oA[#oA + 1] = WA;
                  elseif WA.kind == "ufo" then
                    oA[#oA + 1] = WA;
                  end

            else

          end

        end

        VA.fieldFresh = QA <= 2;
      end

        if not _A[WA] then
          tA[WA] = nil;
        end
      end
      GA = b[20];
      zA = #GA.pick;
      for WA = zA, 1, -1 do
        GA = b[20];

          table.remove(b[20].pick, WA);
        end
      end

      ZA = GA.skip;

          pA = b[20];
          pA.skip[WA] = nil;
        end
      end
      table.sort(oA, function(WA, LA)
        if WA.b ~= LA.b then
          return WA.b > LA.b;
        end
        if WA[RA] ~= LA[RA] then
          return WA[RA] > LA[RA];
        end
        return WA.v > LA.v;
      end);
      return oA;
    end

    local function YA(kA)

    function xA.run(YA, WA)

            end

                  else

                  end
                else
                  CA, sA = SA, NA;

    local function CA(sA)
      local lA = (sA >= 1000 and "%.0f" or "%.2f"):format(sA);
      local sA, SA = lA:match("^(%-?%d+)(%.%d+)$");
      local GA, _A = 1, sA or lA;
      while GA > 0 do
        _A, GA = _A:gsub("^(%-?%d+)(%d%d%d)", "%1,%2");
      end
      return _A .. (SA or "") .. " Kg";
    end
    local function sA(lA, SA)

    end
    local function CA(lA)

        qA, YA = qA:gsub("^(%-?%d+)(%d%d%d)", "%1,%2");

  local function aA(nA, iA)
    local MA = ({Biggest = "s", ["Best rarity"] = "r"})[iA] or "v";
    local YA = ({Smallest = "s", ["Lowest value"] = "v"})[iA];
    table.sort(nA, function(iA, kA)
      if YA then
        return iA[YA] < kA[YA];
      end
      if iA[MA] ~= kA[MA] then
        return iA[MA] > kA[MA];
      end
      return iA.v > kA.v;
    end);
    return nA;
  end

      FA.Silver = DA(255, 255, 255, 160, 176, 200);
      FA.Sakura = DA(255, 214, 236, 255, 110, 190);
      FA.GreatBloom = DA(205, 255, 230, 60, 225, 150);
      FA.Boss = DA(255, 190, 190, 255, 70, 70);
      FA.Monstrous = DA(228, 204, 255, 150, 90, 255);
      FA.Scrambled = DA(215, 255, 160, 40, 215, 170);
      FA.Rainbow = VA;
      FA.Prismatic = VA;
      local VA, HA = DA(215, 245, 255, 80, 190, 255), {};
      HA.ETERNAL = rA(0, 196, 222, 255, 0.45, 246, 242, 255, 1, 214, 180, 255);
      HA.SECRET = rA(0, 240, 243, 250, 0.3, 120, 128, 146, 0.52, 30, 32, 42, 0.74, 110, 118, 136, 1, 248, 250, 255);
      local QA = DA(255, 255, 255, 236, 240, 248);
      local vA = DA(255, 244, 190, 255, 196, 60);
      local hA, TA = DA(235, 250, 255, 110, 200, 255), {};
      TA.Sort = _A[1];
      TA.Spot = true;
      TA.Filter = "All";
      local OA, BA, jA, wA, Ua, qa, fa, Aa, ba, ua, ca, ea, Pa, Ja, xa, Ia, Xa, Ea, aa, na, ia, Ma, Ya, ka = {}, {}, {}, {}, {}, 0, false, 0, 0, 0, 0;
      local function ya(ra, ma)
        M("UICorner", {Parent = ra, CornerRadius = bA(ma, 0)});
        return ra;
      end
      local function ra(ma, Wa, La, Na)
        return M("UIStroke", {Parent = ma, Color = Wa, Thickness = La, Transparency = Na or 0, ApplyStrokeMode = aA.ApplyStrokeMode.Border});
      end
      local function ma(Wa, La, Na)
        return M("UIGradient", {Parent = Wa, Color = La, Rotation = Na or 90});
      end
      local function Wa(La, Na, Da, Ka, da, ta, Ca, sa)
        sa = sa or {};
        sa.Parent, sa.BackgroundTransparency, sa.Position, sa.Size = La, Da or 0, f(Ka, da), f(ta, Ca);
        if Na then
          sa.BackgroundColor3 = Na;
          sa.BorderSizePixel = 0;
        end
        return M("Frame", sa);
      end
      local function La(Na, Da, Ka, da, ta, Ca, sa, la, Sa)
        Sa = Sa or {};
        Sa.Parent, Sa.AnchorPoint, Sa.BackgroundTransparency, Sa.Position, Sa.Size, Sa.ScaleType = Na, R(Da, Ka), 1, f(da, ta), f(Ca, sa), Sa.ScaleType or aA.ScaleType.Fit;
        Sa.Image = la;
        return M("ImageLabel", Sa);
      end
      local function Na(Da, Ka)
        Ka.Parent = Da;
        Ka.AutoButtonColor = false;
        Ka.Text = "";
        return M("TextButton", Ka);
      end
      local function Da(Ka, da, ta, Ca, sa, la)
        local Sa = M("TextLabel", {Parent = Ka, BackgroundTransparency = 1, TextColor3 = nA, TextTruncate = aA.TextTruncate.AtEnd, Text = "", FontFace = iA.RarityFont, Position = f(da, ta), Size = f(Ca, sa)});
        for Ka, da in next, la or {}, nil do
          Sa[Ka] = da;
        end
        M("UIStroke", {Parent = Sa, Thickness = 1.5});
        return Sa;
      end
      local function Ka(da, ta, Ca)
        return M("UIListLayout", {Parent = da, FillDirection = aA.FillDirection.Horizontal, VerticalAlignment = ta and aA.VerticalAlignment.Center, HorizontalAlignment = Ca, SortOrder = pA, Padding = ta and bA(0, ta)});
      end
      local function da(ta, Ca)
        if ta.Text ~= Ca then
          ta.Text = Ca;
        end
      end
      local function ta(Ca, sa)
        if Ca.Visible ~= sa then
          Ca.Visible = sa;
        end
      end
      local function Ca(sa, la)
        la = xA(6, XA(la + 0.5));
        if sa.TextSize ~= la then
          sa.TextSize = la;
          sa:FindFirstChildOfClass("UIStroke").Thickness = xA(1, la * 0.09);
        end
        if sa.AutomaticSize == aA.AutomaticSize.None then
          local Sa = sa:FindFirstChildOfClass("UITextSizeConstraint");
          if Sa then
          else
            Sa = M("UITextSizeConstraint", {Parent = sa, MinTextSize = 1});
            sa.TextScaled = true;
          end
          if Sa.MaxTextSize ~= la then
            Sa.MaxTextSize = la;
          end
        end
      end
      local function sa(la, Sa)
        for Ga = 1, #Sa, 2 do
          Ca(Sa[Ga], la * Sa[Ga + 1]);
        end
      end
      local function la(Sa)
        if wA[Sa] == nil then
          wA[Sa] = false;
          pcall(function()
            local Ga = P.Assets.UI.RarityGradients:FindFirstChild(Sa);
            local Za = Ga and (Ga:IsA("UIGradient") and Ga or Ga:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("UIGradient", true));
            if Za then
              wA[Sa] = Za.Color;
            end
          end);
        end
        return wA[Sa];
      end
      local function wA(Sa, Ga)
        local Za, oa = la(Ga), Sa:FindFirstChild("EdgePaint");
        if not Za then
          if oa then
            oa:Destroy();
          end
          Sa.Color = sA;
          return;
        end
        Sa.Color = nA;
        Sa.Transparency = 0;
        (oa or M("UIGradient", {Parent = Sa, Name = "EdgePaint", Rotation = 45})).Color = Za;
      end
      local function la(Sa, Ga, Za)
        local oa = Sa:FindFirstChild("RarityPaint");
        if oa then
          oa:Destroy();
        end
        M("UIGradient", {Parent = Sa, Name = "RarityPaint", Color = Ga, Rotation = Za});
      end
      local function Sa(Ga)
        local Za = HA[Ga.Up] or Ga.Tint;
        if Za then
          return Za;
        end
        local HA, oa, za, pa = Ga.Gradient, {}, {}, {};
        if HA and HA:IsA("UIGradient") then
          for _a, _a in ipairs(HA.Color.Keypoints) do
            Za = _a.Value;
            if Za.R * 0.299 + Za.G * 0.587 + Za.B * 0.114 >= 0.45 then
              oa[#oa + 1] = Za;
            end
          end
        end
        if #oa == 0 then
          oa[1] = Ga.Color;
        end
        Za = math.min(#oa, 8);
        for HA = 1, Za, 1 do
          za[HA] = oa[math.clamp(XA((HA - 1) * (#oa - 1) / xA(Za - 1, 1) + 1.5), 1, #oa)]:Lerp(nA, 0.36);
        end
        if Za == 1 then
          za[2] = za[1];
        end
        for HA, Za in ipairs(za) do
          pa[HA] = ColorSequenceKeypoint.new((HA - 1) / (#za - 1), Za);
        end
        Ga.Tint = JA(pa);
        return Ga.Tint;
      end
      local function HA(Ga, Za)
        local oa = Ga.Keypoints;
        if Za <= oa[1].Time then
          return oa[1].Value;
        end
        for Ga = 2, #oa, 1 do
          local za, pa = oa[Ga - 1], oa[Ga];
          if Za <= pa.Time then
            Ga = pa.Time - za.Time;
            return za.Value:Lerp(pa.Value, Ga > 0 and (Za - za.Time) / Ga or 0);
          end
        end
        return oa[#oa].Value;
      end
      local function Ga(Za, oa, za)
        local pa, _a, ga = 90, oa.Gradient;
        if oa.Up == "SECRET" then
          ga = za and QA or iA.SecretGradient;
        else
          if _a and _a:IsA("UIGradient") then
            ga, pa = _a.Color, _a.Rotation;
          else
            ga = JA(oa.Color:Lerp(nA, 0.35), oa.Color);
          end
          local QA = za == (math.cos(math.rad(pa)) < -0.3 or math.sin(math.rad(pa)) < -0.3) and 0.5 or 0;
          local oa, za = QA + 0.5, {ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(0, HA(ga, QA))};
          for _a, _a in ipairs(ga.Keypoints) do
            if _a.Time > QA + 0.001 then
              if _a.Time < oa - 0.001 then
                za[#za + 1] = ColorSequenceKeypoint.new((_a.Time - QA) / (oa - QA), _a.Value);
              end
            end
          end
          za[#za + 1] = ColorSequenceKeypoint.new(1, HA(ga, oa));
          ga = JA(za);
        end
        la(Za, ga, pa);
      end
      local function JA(HA, QA)
        local Za, oa = pcall(function()
          local za = b[32];
          return za.Rec.EggIcon(HA);
        end);
        if Za then
          if type(oa) == "string" then
            if oa ~= "" then
              return oa;
            end
          end
        end
        oa = t(tostring(HA.AssetCategory));
        return type(oa) == "table" and type(oa.Egg) == "table" and oa.Egg.Icon and tostring(oa.Egg.Icon) or QA.Icon or "";
      end
      local function HA(QA, ...)
        local Za = b[22];
        local oa = Za("Rec", QA, ...);
        return type(oa) == "number" and oa or nil;
      end
      local function QA()
        local Za = b[32];
        local oa = Za.Egg;
        local za, pa = pcall(function()
          local _a = E;
          return oa.ReadOwnerEggs(_a.UserId);
        end);
        if za then
          if type(pa) ~= "table" then
            return;
          end
        else
          return;
        end
        local _a, ga, Ra = y(), {}, pairs;
        for Va, Fa in Ra(pa) do
          if type(Fa) == "table" then
            local pa, Ra, Ha = NA(Fa.AssetCategory), tonumber(Fa.AssetScale) or 0, fA(Fa.Mutations);
            za = {Id = Va, Info = pa, Scale = Ra, Weight = HA("WeightKg", Fa) or 0, Mutations = Ha, Income = LA(pa, Ra, Ha), Status = "Inventory", Remaining = 1 / 0, Percent = 0, EggIcon = JA(Fa, pa)};
            if Fa.Placement ~= nil then
              Ha, Ra = pcall(oa.IsReadyToHatch, Va);
              if Ha and Ra then
                za.Status = "Ready";
                za.Remaining = 0;
                za.Percent = 100;
              else
                za.Status = "Growing";
                Za = b[32];
                if Za.Rec then
                  pa = b[32];
                  if pa.Rec.GrowthSecondsRemaining then
                    local JA, Za = HA("GrowthSecondsRemaining", Fa, _a, HA("GrowthSpeedMultiplier", Fa) or 1) or 0, HA("GrowthDuration", Fa) or 0;
                    za.Remaining = JA;
                    if Za > 0 then
                      za.Percent = math.clamp(XA((1 - JA / Za) * 100), 0, 100);
                    end
                  end
                end
              end
            end
            ga[#ga + 1] = za;
          end
        end
        return ga;
      end
      local function JA(HA, Za)
        if Za == "" then
          return true;
        end
        local oa = (HA.Info.Name .. " " .. HA.Info.Rarity .. " " .. HA.Status):lower();
        for za, za in ipairs(HA.Mutations) do
          oa ..= " " .. tostring(za):lower();
        end
        return oa:find(Za, 1, true) ~= nil;
      end
      local function HA(Za, oa, za)
        local pa, _a = "";
        if #oa > 0 then
          local ga = {};
          for Ra, Va in ipairs(oa) do
            ga[Ra] = tostring(D[Va] or Va):gsub("%s*[Mm]utation$", ""):upper();
          end
          pa, _a = table.concat(ga, za or " + "), FA[tostring(oa[1])] or VA;
        else
        end
        da(Za, pa);
        if _a then
          if Za:GetAttribute("MutationKey") ~= pa then
            Za:SetAttribute("MutationKey", pa);
            (Za:FindFirstChild("MutationPaint") or M("UIGradient", {Parent = Za, Name = "MutationPaint", Rotation = 90})).Color = _a;
          end
        end
        return pa ~= "";
      end
      local function VA(FA, Za, oa, za, pa, _a)
        local ga = Wa(FA, nil, 1, Za, oa, za, pa);
        local function FA(Za, oa)
          local za = Da(ga, 0, 0, 0, 1, {AutomaticSize = oA, LayoutOrder = Za, TextTruncate = ZA});
          ma(za, oa);
          return za;

        return {Layout = Ka(ga, 8, _a), Size = FA(1, vA), Weight = FA(2, hA)};
      end
      local function FA(vA, hA, Za, oa, za)

        ra(pa, dA, 1.5);

        return {Fill = za, Gradient = hA, Text = Da(pa, 0, 0, 1, 1, {ZIndex = 3}), Alpha = -1};
      end
      local function vA(hA, Za, oa)

      end
      local function hA(Za, oa, za, pa, _a, ga)

      end
      local function za(pa)
        local _a = pa.Stats;
        sa(ua, {pa.Rank, 0.062, pa.Mutation, 0.06, pa.Name, 0.078, pa.Rarity, 0.056, pa.Value, 0.082, _a.Size, 0.062, _a.Weight, 0.062, pa.Progress.Text, 0.062});

      end
      local function pa(_a, ga)
        local Ra, Va, Fa = ga.Info, tostring(ga.Id) .. "|" .. ga.Info.Category, false;
        if _a.Key ~= Va then
          _a.Key = Va;
          la(_a.Art, Sa(Ra), 90);
          Ga(_a.Name, Ra, true);
          Ga(_a.Rarity, Ra, false);
          wA(_a.Stroke, Ra.Up == "DIVINE" and "Divine" or "Secret");
          _a.Image.Image, _a.Corner.Image = Ra.Icon or ga.EggIcon, ga.EggIcon;
          da(_a.Name, Ra.Name);
          da(_a.Rarity, Ra.Up);
          Fa = true;
        else
        end
        ta(_a.CornerBack, ga.EggIcon ~= "" and Ra.Icon ~= nil);
        return Fa;
      end
      local function wA(la, Sa)
        local Ga, _a, ga = la.Progress, RA[Sa.Status], Sa.Status == "Growing" and math.clamp(Sa.Percent / 100, 0.04, 1) or 1;
        da(la.Value, "$" .. mA(Sa.Income));
        da(la.Stats.Size, ("x%.2f"):format(Sa.Scale));
        da(la.Stats.Weight, WA(Sa.Weight));
        if Ga.Style ~= _a then
          Ga.Style = _a;
          Ga.Gradient.Color = _a[1];
        end
        if math.abs(ga - Ga.Alpha) > 0.001 then
          Ga.Alpha = ga;
          Ga.Fill.Size = f(ga, 1);
        end
        ga, _a = Ga.Text, Sa.Status == "Ready" and "READY" or Sa.Status ~= "Growing" and "IN BAG";
        da(ga, _a or Sa.Remaining == 1 / 0 and "GROWING" or W(Sa.Remaining) .. "  " .. Sa.Percent .. "%");
      end
      local function la(Sa, Ga, _a)
        Sa.Id = Ga.Id;
        ta(Sa.Frame, true);
        pa(Sa, Ga);
        da(Sa.Rank, "#" .. _a);
        HA(Sa.Mutation, Ga.Mutations, "\10");
        _a = f(0.64, 0.07 * xA(1, #Ga.Mutations));
        if Sa.Mutation.Size ~= _a then
          Sa.Mutation.Size = _a;
        end
        wA(Sa, Ga);
        ta(Sa.Select, Ga.Id == ea);
      end
      local function Sa()
        local Ga = aa.Focus;
        if not Ga or ka then
          return;
        end
        ka = true;
        local _a = tostring(Ga.Id);
        pcall(function()
          if Ga.Status == "Inventory" then
            if not pcall(function()
              local ga = b[32];
              return ga.Egg.WearEggTool(_a);
            end) then
              local ga = b[9];
              ga("RF/EggWorld/AskWearTool", _a);
            end
          elseif Ga.Status == "Ready" then
            local Ga = b[72];
            if Ga("RF/EggWorld/AskHatch", _a) then
              task.wait(0.35);
              local Ga = b[9];
              Ga("RF/EggWorld/AskFinishHatch", _a);
            end
          else
            local Ga = workspace:FindFirstChild("PlacedEggRenders");
            local ga;
            local Ra, Va = ipairs, Ga and Ga:GetChildren() or {};
            for Fa, Fa in Ra(Va) do
              if Fa.Name:find(_a, 1, true) or Fa:GetAttribute("Uid") == _a then
                ga = Fa;
                break;
              end
            end
            Ra, Ga = pcall(function()
              return ga:IsA("Model") and ga:GetPivot() or ga.CFrame;
            end);
            if Ra then
              MA = Ga.Position;
            end
          end
        end);
        ka, YA = false, true;
      end
      local function MA(ka)
        aa = {};
        local Ga, _a, ga = ya(Wa(nil, tA, 0.04, 0, 0, 1, 1, {Visible = false}), 0.1), aa, aa;

        Ka(Ra, 10);
        ga = aa;
        ga.Name = Da(Ra, 0, 0, 0, 1, {AutomaticSize = oA, LayoutOrder = 1, TextXAlignment = GA});
        ga = aa;
        ga.NameLimit = M("UISizeConstraint", {Parent = ga.Name, MaxSize = R(400, 400)});
        ga = aa;
        ga.Rarity = Da(Ra, 0, 0.235, 0, 1, {AutomaticSize = oA, LayoutOrder = 2, TextTruncate = ZA, TextXAlignment = GA});
        ga = aa;
        ga.Mutation = Da(_a, 0, 0.235, 1, 0.12, {TextTruncate = ZA, TextXAlignment = GA});

        ga.Value = Da(_a, 0, 0.36, 1, 0.2, {TextXAlignment = GA});

        ga = aa;

        ya(Ra, 0.24);
        ra(Ra, dA, 1.5, 0.2);

        _a.ActionText = Da(Ra, 0.5, 0.5, 1.02, 1.12, {AnchorPoint = R(0.5, 0.5), TextTruncate = ZA, ZIndex = 21});
        local function aA()
          task.spawn(Sa);
        end
        Ra.Activated:Connect(aA);
        Pa:Attach(Ga, ka);
      end
      local function aA(lA, ZA, oA)
        local VA = TA.Spot and lA ~= nil;
        ta(aa.Frame, VA);
        aa.Focus = VA and lA or nil;
        local FA = VA and 7.6 or 0;
        if aa.Dock ~= FA then
          aa.Dock = FA;
          Pa:SetDock(FA, {Gap = 0.35, Divider = false});
        end
        if not VA then
          return;
        end
        if pa(aa, lA) then
          VA = lA.Info.Up == "SECRET";
          aa.Shade.Transparency = vA(0.5, VA and 0.92 or 0.82, VA and 0.72 or 0.25);
        end
        FA, VA = HA(aa.Mutation, lA.Mutations), aa.Rarity;
        ta(aa.Mutation, FA);
        local HA = FA and aa.NameRow or aa.Body;
        if VA.Parent ~= HA then
          VA.Parent, VA.Size = HA, f(0, FA and 1 or 0.12);
        end
        wA(aa, lA);
        FA = RA[lA.Status];
        if aa.ActionStyle ~= FA then
          VA = aa;
          VA.ActionStyle, VA.ActionGradient.Color = FA, FA[1];
          da(aa.ActionText, FA[2]);
        end
        da(aa.Rank, ("#%d of %d eggs  by %s"):format(ZA, oA, TA.Sort:lower()));
      end
      local function lA(ZA, oA)
        if ZA <= 0 then
          return;
        elseif oA <= 0 then
          return;

          if ba == oA then
            return;
          end

      local function R(qA)
        Pa = qA;

      local function f()
        local qA;
        task.spawn(function()
          local bA, MA = pcall(QA);
          if bA then
            qA = MA;
          end

        if qA then
          Ua, qA = qA, qA;

          qA = Ua;

          bA[GA.Status] += 1;
          lA, SA = lA + GA.Income, if SA then if GA.Income > SA.Income then GA else SA else GA;

          da(BA[GA].Value, tostring(bA[ZA[1]]));

            GA.Active, GA.Gradient.Enabled, GA.Button.BackgroundColor3, GA.Button.BackgroundTransparency, GA.Text.TextColor3, GA.Stroke.Color = lA, lA, lA and nA or tA, lA and 0 or 0.15, lA and nA or CA, lA and dA or sA;
          end
        end
        table.sort(qA, function(dA, tA)
          if MA == _A[2] then
            if dA.Info.RarityNumber ~= tA.Info.RarityNumber then
              return dA.Info.RarityNumber > tA.Info.RarityNumber;
            end
          end
          if MA == _A[3] then
            if dA.Remaining ~= tA.Remaining then
              return dA.Remaining < tA.Remaining;
            end
          end
          if dA.Income ~= tA.Income then
            return dA.Income > tA.Income;
          end
          return tostring(dA.Id) < tostring(tA.Id);
        end);
        local MA, dA, tA = Pa:Query():lower(), {}, qA;
        for CA, CA in ipairs(tA) do
          if TA.Filter == "All" or CA.Status == TA.Filter then
            if JA(CA, MA) then
              dA[#dA + 1] = CA;
            end
          end
        end
        tA, bA = qA;
        DA = 0;
        for JA, CA in ipairs(tA) do
          if CA.Id == ea then
            bA, DA = CA, JA;
            break;
          end
          if CA == SA then
            bA, DA = CA, JA;
          else
          end
        end
        aA(bA, DA, #qA);
        DA, bA = #dA, 0;
        for JA = 1, DA, 1 do
          tA = OA[JA];
          if tA then
          else
            if bA >= 12 then
              YA = true;
              DA = JA - 1;
              break;

            tA = oa(JA);
            OA[JA] = tA;
            if ua > 0 then
              za(tA);
            end
          end
          la(tA, dA[JA], JA);
        end
        for JA = DA + 1, #OA, 1 do
          ta(OA[JA].Frame, false);
          OA[JA].Id = nil;

        da(tA, bA or MA ~= "" and ("No eggs match \"%s\""):format(MA) or "No eggs in this filter");
        ta(Ea, Ea.Text ~= "");
      end
      local qA, bA = cA["Egg Predictor"], PA.Predictor.page;
      qA:Dropdown("Sort By", _A, _A[1], function(PA)
        if table.find(_A, PA) then
          TA.Sort = PA;
          YA = true;

      qA:Toggle("Preview Card", true, function(PA)
        TA.Spot = PA == true;
        YA = true;

          YA = true;

      n(E, function(R)
        local qA = iA.IsShown(bA) and bA.AbsoluteSize.X > 0;
        local bA = qA and Ja ~= nil and iA.IsShown(Ja);

          if not fa then
            YA = true;
          end

          return;

    end
    do
      local f, R, qA, bA, PA, JA, aA, MA = "#9FB8FF", "#4DFF7A", "#FFC24D", "#FFFFFF", "#CDE7FF", "#FFDF8A", 2.9, 0.0909;
      local DA = iA.Paint;
      local dA = iA.Bold;
      local tA = iA.Escape;
      local CA = iA.MutationText;
      local sA = iA.Separator();
      local lA, SA, GA, ZA = rA(0, 255, 255, 255, 0.5, 222, 238, 255, 1, 255, 255, 255), 3.15, {0, "#8F98A8", 0.3, "#C6CDDA", 0.85, "#FFFFFF", 1.45, "#7CFF9E", 1.9, "#4FE0FF", 2.85, "#6FA0FF", 3.8, "#C08BFF", 5.8, "#FF9A3D", 9.5, "#FF5C5C", 12, "#FFD34D", 20, "#FF4DE8"};
      local rA, oA, zA, pA, _A, gA, RA = {}, {}, {}, {}, 0, 0, 0.06;

      local FA = -1;

      local BA, jA = 0.01, 150;

        return dA(DA(Ua, qa));
      end

        return ("%.2fx"):format(Ua);
      end
      local function Ua(qa)
        return ("%.2fx - %.2fx"):format(qa.Min, qa.Max);
      end
      local function qa(fa, Aa)
        local ba = b[22];
        local ua = ba("Rec", "WeightKgForScale", fa, Aa);
        return type(ua) == "number" and ua > 0 and ua or nil;
      end
      local function fa(Aa)
        return Aa and sA .. DA(PA, WA(Aa)) or "";
      end
      local function Aa(ba)
        local ua = "#FFFFFF";
        for ca = 1, #GA, 2 do
          ua = if ba + 0.001 >= GA[ca] then GA[ca + 1] else ua;
        end
        return ua;
      end
      local function GA()
        if OA then
          local ba = OA;
          return ba;
        end
        local ba, ua = pcall(function()
          local ca = b[32];
          return (debug.getupvalues or getupvalues)(ca.Rec.DrawAssetScale);
        end);
        local ca, ea = pairs, ba and type(ua) == "table" and ua or {};
        for Pa, Ja in ca(ea) do
          if type(Ja) == "table" then
            if type(Ja.SCALE_BANDS) == "table" then
              ba, ua, Pa = Ja.SCALE_BANDS, tonumber(Ja.SCALE_DOUBLING_ODDS) or BA, tonumber(Ja.SCALE_HARD_CAP) or jA;
              OA, BA, jA = ba, ua, Pa;
              break;
            end
            if type(Ja[1]) == "table" then
              if Ja[1].min then
                if Ja[1].weight then
                  OA = Ja;
                  break;
                end
              end
            end
          end
        end
        if not OA then
          ca = {0.85, 1.05, 2000, 1.45, 1.55, 250, 1.9, 2.1, 125, 2.85, 3.15, 62.5, 3.8, 4.2, 31.25, 0.3, 0.45, 18, 0.1, 0.2, 5, 5.8, 6.2, 15.625, 9.5, 12.5, 3, 12, 17, 0.05, 20, 35, 1.0E-4};
          OA = {};
          ua = #ca;
          for ea = 1, ua, 3 do
            ba = OA;
            ba[#ba + 1] = {min = ca[ea], max = ca[ea + 1], weight = ca[ea + 2]};
          end
        end
        ba = OA;
        return ba;
      end
      local function OA(ba, ua, ca)
        local ea = b[22];
        local Pa = ea("Fuse", "BandWeightBias", ba, ua, ca);
        if type(Pa) == "number" then
          return Pa;
        end
        Pa = 0.6931471805599453;
        return math.exp(math.log((ba[1] + ba[2] + ba[3]) / 3) / Pa * (math.log((ua + ca) / 2) / Pa) * 0.6);
      end
      local function ba()
        local ua = b[64];
        local ca = ua();
        if not ca then
          return;
        end
        local ea, Pa, Ja, xa = fA(ca.FusionSlots), fA(ca.Inventory), {}, ca.FusionEggReward;

            Ja[#Ja + 1] = {Category = ua.Category, Scale = tonumber(ua.Scale) or 1, Mutations = fA(ua.Mutations), Raw = ua};

      end
      local function ua(ca)

        if gA > 0 then
          if ea.Text == tostring(ca.Spec.Text or "") then
            return ea;
          end
        end
        hA = true;
      end
      local function ca(ea)
        local Pa = ua(ea);

          return;

        Pa.Size = z(100000, xA(1, Ja.Y.Offset));

        Pa.TextWrapped = xa;

          Pa = gA;
          return ea / Pa;
        end

      end

        local xa = ua(Pa);

      end
      local function z(XA, ua, Pa, Ja, xa)

      local function nA(Pa)

      local function Pa()
        if _A <= 0 then
          return;
        end
        hA = false;

          vA = xa + 0.1;

              aA.SU = xa + 0.23;

            xa = ca(aA.Rarity);
            if xa then
              aA.RU = xa + 0.1;
            end

            xa = ca(aA.Name);
            if xa then
              aA.NU = xa + 0.1;

            aA.Status.Set({X = SA + Xa - vA, Y = ea, Width = xA(0.5, vA)});
            Ia += xa + 0.22;
          end
        end
        Ia = xA(1, Ia);
        if math.abs(Ia - HA) > 0.01 then
          HA = Ia;

        end
      end
      local function aA(SA, HA)

        ea(Ja);
      end
      kA = function()
        if not ZA then
          return;
        end
        TA = 2;
        table.clear(pA);
        local iA, kA = 0, 0;
        local function SA(HA, QA)
          iA += 1;
          local vA = zA[iA];
          if vA then
          else
            vA = ZA:Text({Name = "Line", Width = 1, Height = 1, Wrap = true, Visible = false});
            zA[iA] = vA;
          end
          vA.Set({Visible = true, Text = HA});
          pA[#pA + 1] = {Handle = vA, Gap = QA};
        end
        local function HA(QA, vA)
          SA(wA(vA, QA), #pA > 0);

    local function XA(aA)

        return false;

      local function GA(BA)

        while math.abs(BA) >= 1000 and Pa < #K do
          BA, Pa = BA / 1000, Pa + 1;
        end
        return (Pa == 0 and "%.0f" or "%.2f"):format(BA) .. (K[Pa] or "");

    CA.reclaim = function()
      CA.sw = CA.sw or {};

      for L, D in m(W) do
        local K = L.Parent;
        if K then
          local d, o = CA.mask(D[1]), CA.sw[L];

            if o.Parent ~= K then

            end
          else
          end
          if o then
          else

            if CA.txOn then
              if L.Text ~= d then
                local z = L:Clone();
                z.Name = "t" .. math.random(1000000000);
                z.RichText = true;
                z.Text = d;
                z.Visible = true;
                z.TextTransparency = 0;
                for g, g in ipairs(z:GetChildren()) do
                  if g:IsA("UIStroke") then
                    g.Enabled = true;
                  end
                end
                for g, g in ipairs(L:GetChildren()) do
                  if g:IsA("UIStroke") then
                    g.Enabled = false;
                  end
                end
                z.Parent, CA.sw[L] = K, z;
                o = z;
              else
                if L.Text ~= d then
                  L.Text = d;
                end
              end
            else
              if L.Text ~= d then
                L.Text = d;
              end
            end
            if not CA.txOn then
              L.Visible = D[2];
            end
          end
          if o then
            if o.Text ~= d then
              o.Text = d;
            end
            local D = L ~= Wa.stat or L.Visible;
            if o.Visible ~= D then
              o.Visible = D;
            end
            if L.TextTransparency ~= 1 then
              L.TextTransparency = 1;
            end
          end
        end
      end

    local U = {Mutate = true, Bloom = true, Combat = true, Machine = true, Banjo = true};
    for q, q in ipairs(j) do
      if U[q.name] then
        q.vis = true;
      end
    end
    local function U(q)
      local f = b[20];
      return f.rank[q.name] or q.pri or 9;
    end
    local function q(f)
      local A, u = pcall(f.on);
      return A and u;
    end
    local function f()
      table.sort(j, function(A, u)
        local c, e = A.mover and U(A) or 99, u.mover and U(u) or 99;
        if c ~= e then
          return c < e;
        end
        return A.i < u.i;
      end);
    end
    local A, u = b[20], b[20];
    A.order = f;
    u.rank2 = U;
    f();
    local function f(A)
      local u = b[20];
      local c, e = u.lock;
      if c then
        u = b[20];
        e = u.lock();
      else
        e = c;
      end
      return e ~= nil and A.name ~= e;
    end
    local function A()
      local u = b[20];
      if u.invisBusy then
        local c, e = b[20], b[20];
        c.busyAt = e.busyAt or os.clock();
        e, c = b[20], os.clock() - b[20].busyAt;
        if c > 20 then
          local c, e = b[20], b[20];
          c.invisBusy = false;
          e.busyAt = nil;
        end
      else
        local c = b[20];
        c.busyAt = nil;
      end
      u = b[20];
      local c, e = u.invisBusy;
      if c then
        e = c;
      else
        u = nil;
        u, e = b[20], os.clock() < (b[20].invisSettle or 0);
      end
      return e;
    end
    local u = b[20];
    u.needVis = function(u, c)
      local e = u.vis;
      return (if type(e) == "function" then e(c) else e) == true;
    end;
    local function u(c)
      return c.rank or U(c);
    end
    local U = b[20];
    U.better = function(U)
      local c = b[20];
      for e, e in ipairs(c.queue or {}) do
        if e ~= U then
          if not e.aux then
            return u(e) < math.max(U.erank or 0, u(U));
          end
        end
      end
      return false;
    end;
    local function U(c, e, P)

          e = b[20];

        end

          x.busy = false;
          if b[4] == x then
            b[4] = nil;
          end
        end
      end

          e.at = math.max(e.at, os.clock() + 2);
        end
        if e.flipped then
          if not e.acted then
            if os.clock() - e.t0 < 8 then
              e.at = math.max(e.at, os.clock() + 2);
            end
          end
        end

        X = P();

          if U.PlatformStand then
            x = b[20];
            if x.lock then
              P = b[20];
              if not P.lock() then
                U.PlatformStand = false;
              end
            else
              U.PlatformStand = false;
            end
          end
        end
        if X then
          if not X.Anchored then
            x = E;
            X.AssemblyLinearVelocity = x;
          end
        end
        X, U = b[20], b[20];
        e.busy, e.idle, e.plan, X.mvEnd = false, nil, nil, os.clock();
        U.lastMV = e;
        if b[4] == e then
          b[4] = nil;
        end
      end);
    end
    local function U(e)
      if e.ready then
        local P, x = pcall(e.ready);
        return P and x;
      end
      return e.lastWant ~= false;
    end
    local function e(P, x)
      local I, X = u(P), u(x);
      if I ~= X then
        return I < X;
      end
      return P.i < x.i;
    end
    local function P()

      I.schedTok = x;

                        m.rank = nil;
                        Y[#Y + 1] = m;
                      end
                    end
                  end
                end
              end
            end
          end
          table.sort(I, e);
          table.sort(Y, e);

          U.queue = I;
          e.queueAt = x;
          e = A();

            for A, k in y(Y) do
              I, U = b[4], f(k);
              local f, Y = not U;
              if U then
                Y = f;
              else
                local r, m = not I;
                if I then

                  if W then
                    L = W;
                  else
                    local W, N = k.preempt;
                    if W then
                      N = W;
                    else
                      local D, K = k.name == "Steal";
                      if D then
                        A = b[20];
                        K = A.dawnNow;
                      else
                        K = D;
                      end
                      if K then
                        D = b[20];
                        N = D.dawnNow();
                      else
                        N = K;
                      end
                    end
                    if N then
                      local D, K = I.hold;
                      if D then
                        W = b[21];
                        K = W();
                      else
                        K = D;
                      end
                      L = not K;
                    else
                      L = N;
                    end
                  end
                  m = L and u(k) < I.erank;
                else
                  m = r;
                end
                Y = m or k.urgent and not I.hold;
              end
              if Y then
                if not k.aux then
                  A = b[20];
                  if A.stealDawn then
                    f = b[20];
                    if f.stealDawn(u(k)) then
                      break;
                    end
                  end
                end
                if x < k.at then
                  if k.inq then
                    break;
                  end
                  continue;
                elseif k.busy then
                  if k.inq then
                    break;
                  end
                  continue;
                end
                k.at = x + k.gap;
                local f, A = pcall(k.want);
                if f then
                  if A == "wait" then
                    break;
                  end
                end
                k.lastWant = f and A ~= nil and A ~= false;
                if k.lastWant then
                  if b[4] == I then
                    c(k, A);
                    break;
                  end
                end
              end
            end
          end
          y = ipairs;
          for f, f in y(j) do
            I, U = n and not f.mover;
            if I then
              e = f.busy;
              U = if e then x - (f.bAt or x) > 30 else not e;
            else
              U = I;
            end
            if U then
              if x >= f.at then
                if f.on then
                  if q(f) then
                    f.at = x + f.gap;
                    f.bAt = x;
                    f.busy = true;
                    task.spawn(function()
                      pcall(f.fn);
                      f.busy = false;
                    end);
                  end
                end
              end
            end
          end
        end, debug.traceback);
        if not X then
          local U = b[20];
          U.schedErr = M;
          U = b[11];
          U("Scheduler: " .. tostring(M):sub(1, 60), "err");
        end
        task.wait(0.25);
      end
    end
    local function U()
      local q, f = xpcall(P, debug.traceback);
      if not q then
        local q = b[20];
        q.schedErr = "outer: " .. tostring(f);
      end
    end
    task.spawn(U);
    n(E, function()
      if n then
        local q, q = b[20], os.clock() - (b[20].queueAt or os.clock());
        if q > 15 then
          local q, q = b[20], os.clock() - (b[20].schedAt or 0);
          if q > 15 then
            local q = b[20];
            q.schedAt = os.clock();
            q = b[11];
            q("Scheduler restarted", "warn");
            task.spawn(U);
          end
        end
      end
    end);
    local U = w({name = "Guard", gap = 0.5});
    U.on = function()
      local q, f = b[4] ~= nil and b[4].name ~= "Treadmill";
      if q then
        local A = b[20];
        f = (A.hold or 0) == 0;
      else
        f = q;
      end
      q = nil;
      if f then
        local A = b[20];
        q = not A.lock();
      else
        q = f;
      end
      return q;
    end;
    U.fn = function()
      local q = b[15];
      local f = q();
      if f then
        if f.Anchored then
          U.n = (U.n or 0) + 1;
          if U.n >= 2 then
            U.n = 0;
            b[13](function()
              return true;
            end);
          end
        else
          U.n = 0;
        end
      else
        U.n = 0;
      end
    end;
    local U = w({name = "Info", gap = 1});
    U.on = function()
      return true;
    end;
    U.fn = function()
      local U = b[20];
      if U.tick then
        local U = b[20];
        H("tick", U.tick);
      end
    end;
  end);
  a.ChilliHubSaeCleanup = function()
    local U = E;
    local q, f = U.Character;
    if q then
      U = E;
      f = U.Character:GetAttribute("InvisApplied");
    else
      f = q;
    end
    n = false;
    q = b[20];
    for U, U in ipairs(q.cleanup or {}) do
      pcall(U);
    end
    for U, U in ipairs(i) do
      pcall(U.Disconnect, U);
    end
    q = b[25];
    q.god = false;
    pcall(IA);
    q = b[8];
    if q() then
      pcall(EA, b[8]());
    end
    pcall(V.Destroy, V);
    if a.StopChilliLink then
      pcall(a.StopChilliLink);
    end
    a.ChilliHubSaeCleanup = nil;
    task.spawn(function()
      for U = 1, 2, 1 do
        task.wait(0.5);
        U = b[9];
        U("RF/Treadmill/AskDoff");
      end
      if f then
        local U = b[20];
        U.respawn(true);
      end
    end);
  end;
  V:Finish();
  if #J > 0 then
    pcall(writefile, "ChilliLibrary/SAE_LoadErrors.txt", os.date("!%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S") .. " SAE v2020\10" .. table.concat(J, "\10"));
  end
  pcall(writefile, "ChilliLibrary/SAE_LoadPart.txt", "done");
  pcall(getgenv().ChilliHubLoadStep, 10);
  task.delay(1, pcall, function()
    loadstring(game:HttpGet("https://raw.githubusercontent.com/tienkhanh1/spicy/refs/heads/main/DiscordLink"))();
  end);
end

return {
  Loading = LoadingScreen,
  UI = UILib,
  Main = Main,
}
